library?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse the CLI\u003c/strong\u003e if you already have an agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Hermes, OpenClaw, etc.) that you want to complete browser tasks for you. The agent installs the skill once (see \u003ca href=\"#quickstart\"\u003eQuickstart\u003c/a\u003e) and can then control the browser. Examples:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\"Upload this video to YouTube\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\"Compare these three laptops and give me a table with prices\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\"Fill in this job application with my resume\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse the Python library\u003c/strong\u003e when you are building software that automates the web. Examples:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun many tasks on a schedule or in parallel (scraping, monitoring, QA)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEmbed a browser agent into your own product\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustom tools, custom system prompts, structured output, fine-grained browser control\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRule of thumb: one-off tasks through an agent → CLI. Repeatable automation in code → Python library.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eWhat's the best model to use?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWe optimized \u003cstrong\u003eChatBrowserUse()\u003c/strong\u003e specifically for browser automation tasks. On avg it completes tasks 3-5x faster than other models with SOTA accuracy.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor pricing and other LLM providers, see our \u003ca href=\"https://docs.browser-use.com/supported-models\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003esupported models documentation\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eCan I use Claude / GPT / Gemini through ChatBrowserUse?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYes. \u003ccode\u003eChatBrowserUse\u003c/code\u003e accepts provider-prefixed model ids, so a single \u003ccode\u003eBROWSER_USE_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e reaches all of them — no separate OpenAI/Anthropic/Google keys required:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from browser_use import Agent, ChatBrowserUse\n\nllm = ChatBrowserUse(model='anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6')  # or 'openai/gpt-5.5', 'google/gemini-3-pro'\nagent = Agent(task='...', llm=llm)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ebrowser_use\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAgent\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eChatBrowserUse\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ellm\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eChatBrowserUse\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6'\u003c/span\u003e)  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# or 'openai/gpt-5.5', 'google/gemini-3-pro'\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eagent\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eAgent\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etask\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'...'\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ellm\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ellm\u003c/span\u003e)\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor the best speed and cost we still recommend the default \u003ccode\u003ebu-*\u003c/code\u003e models.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eShould I use the Browser Use system prompt with the open-source preview model?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYes. If you use \u003ccode\u003eChatBrowserUse(model='browser-use/bu-30b-a3b-preview')\u003c/code\u003e with a normal \u003ccode\u003eAgent(...)\u003c/code\u003e, Browser Use still sends its default agent system prompt for you.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYou do \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e need to add a separate custom \"Browser Use system message\" just because you switched to the open-source preview model. Only use \u003ccode\u003eextend_system_message\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003eoverride_system_message\u003c/code\u003e when you intentionally want to customize the default behavior for your task.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you want the best default speed/accuracy, we still recommend the newer hosted \u003ccode\u003ebu-*\u003c/code\u003e models. If you want the open-source preview model, the setup stays the same apart from the \u003ccode\u003emodel=\u003c/code\u003e value.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eCan I use custom tools with the agent?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYes! Cloud\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e","\u003c/div\u003e","","\u003c/br\u003e","","# What can Browser Use do?","","Browser Use lets an AI agent use a web browser the same way humans do — it opens pages, clicks buttons, types, and fills in forms. You describe the task, and it completes it. For example, you can have it:","","","### 📋 Fill Forms","#### Task: \"Fill in this job application with my resume and information.\"","","![Job Application Demo](https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/57611d8e-0474-4de6-84b7-37a0c0cd27e7)","","[Example code ↗](https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/blob/main/examples/use-cases/apply_to_job.py)","","","### 🍎 Extract data","#### Task: \"Extract structured data about my followers and export it as a CSV.\"","","https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/485fd3ec-61b9-4afc-9e86-ee9b85acb592","","[Browser Use Cloud Docs ↗](https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/quickstart)","","","\u003cbr/\u003e","","# Start with Browser Use Cloud","","Eligible new Google, GitHub or Microsoft signups get a one-time **$15 Cloud credit**. No card required; email/password signups do not qualify. [Pricing and eligibility](https://browser-use.com/pricing.md).","","- **Hosted agent:** [V4 quickstart](https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/agent/quickstart), using `browser-use-sdk`.","- **Your own agent:** [Managed browser quickstart](https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/browser/quickstart).","- **Local framework:** the `browser-use` Python library below.","","# Quickstart","","If you want to use Browser Use in your agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Hermes, OpenClaw, etc.), paste this prompt, and it sets everything up itself:","","```text","Install or upgrade browser-use to the latest stable version with uv using Python 3.12, run `browser-use skill install` to register the skill, and connect it to my browser. If setup or connection fails, follow https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/install.md.","```","","Then tell your agent what you want done.","","\u003cbr/\u003e","","# Python library: the easiest way to automate the web","","Want to automate the web at scale, from your own code, and with any LLM? Use the Python library:","","**1. Install Browser Use (Python \u003e= 3.11):**","","```bash","uv add browser-use","# or: pip install browser-use","```","","**2. Add your LLM API key to `.env`**. Get one from [Browser Use Cloud](https://cloud.browser-use.com/new-api-key?utm_source=github\u0026utm_medium=readme-quickstart-api-key), or bring your own provider key:","","```bash","# .env","BROWSER_USE_API_KEY=your-key","# GOOGLE_API_KEY=your-key","# ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=your-key","```","","**3. Run your first agent:**","","```python","import asyncio","","from browser_use import Agent, ChatBrowserUse","","async def main():","    agent = Agent(","        task=\"Find the number of stars of the browser-use repo\",","        llm=ChatBrowserUse(model='openai/gpt-5.5'),","        # llm=ChatBrowserUse(model='bu-2-0-mini-preview'),  # Browser Use's optimized model","        # llm=ChatOpenAI(model='gpt-5.5'),","        # llm=ChatAnthropic(model='claude-opus-4-8'),  # Sonnet also works well","    )","    history = await agent.run()","","if __name__ == \"__main__\":","    asyncio.run(main())","```","","Check out the [library docs](https://docs.browser-use.com/open-source/introduction) and the [cloud docs](https://docs.cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github\u0026utm_medium=readme-cloud-docs) for more!","","\u003cbr/\u003e","","# Open Source vs Cloud","","\u003cpicture\u003e","  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"static/accuracy_by_model_light.png\"\u003e","  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"static/accuracy_by_model_dark.png\"\u003e","  \u003cimg alt=\"BU Bench V1 - LLM Success Rates\" src=\"static/accuracy_by_model_light.png\" width=\"100%\"\u003e","\u003c/picture\u003e","","We benchmark Browser Use across 100 real-world browser tasks. Full benchmark is open source: **[browser-use/benchmark](https://github.com/browser-use/benchmark)**.","","Browser Use is also **#1 on the [Odysseys leaderboard](https://odysseysbench.com/leaderboard)** with an 87.4% average, ahead of computer-use agents from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and Microsoft. Odysseys measures the agent's performance on 200 long-horizon web tasks.","","**Use the Open-Source Agent**","- Free, and runs on your own machine","- Deep code-level integration and control: pick your LLM, customize the agent's behavior","- We recommend pairing it with our [cloud browsers](https://docs.browser-use.com/open-source/customize/browser/remote) for leading stealth, proxy rotation, and scaling","","**Use the [Fully-Hosted Cloud Agent](https://cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github\u0026utm_medium=readme-hosted-agent) (recommended)**","- Much more powerful agent for complex tasks (see plot above)","- Easiest way to start and scale","- Best stealth with proxy rotation and captcha solving","- 1000+ integrations (Gmail, Slack, Notion, and more)","- Persistent filesystem and memory","- Rerunnable scripts fetch live data, even when sites change ([guide](https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/agent/scripts))","","```sh","curl -X POST https://api.browser-use.com/api/v4/runs \\","  -H \"X-Browser-Use-API-Key: $BROWSER_USE_API_KEY\" \\","  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\","  -d '{\"task\": \"Your task\"}'","```","","\u003cbr/\u003e","","## Integrations, hosting, custom tools, MCP, and more on our [Docs ↗](https://docs.browser-use.com)","","\u003cbr/\u003e","","# FAQ","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eShould I use the CLI vs. the Python library?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","**Use the CLI** if you already have an agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Hermes, OpenClaw, etc.) that you want to complete browser tasks for you. The agent installs the skill once (see [Quickstart](#quickstart)) and can then control the browser. Examples:","- \"Upload this video to YouTube\"","- \"Compare these three laptops and give me a table with prices\"","- \"Fill in this job application with my resume\"","","**Use the Python library** when you are building software that automates the web. Examples:","- Run many tasks on a schedule or in parallel (scraping, monitoring, QA)","- Embed a browser agent into your own product","- Custom tools, custom system prompts, structured output, fine-grained browser control","","Rule of thumb: one-off tasks through an agent → CLI. Repeatable automation in code → Python library.","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eWhat's the best model to use?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","We optimized **ChatBrowserUse()** specifically for browser automation tasks. On avg it completes tasks 3-5x faster than other models with SOTA accuracy.","","For pricing and other LLM providers, see our [supported models documentation](https://docs.browser-use.com/supported-models).","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eCan I use Claude / GPT / Gemini through ChatBrowserUse?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","Yes. `ChatBrowserUse` accepts provider-prefixed model ids, so a single `BROWSER_USE_API_KEY` reaches all of them — no separate OpenAI/Anthropic/Google keys required:","","```python","from browser_use import Agent, ChatBrowserUse","","llm = ChatBrowserUse(model='anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6')  # or 'openai/gpt-5.5', 'google/gemini-3-pro'","agent = Agent(task='...', llm=llm)","```","","For the best speed and cost we still recommend the default `bu-*` models.","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eShould I use the Browser Use system prompt with the open-source preview model?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","Yes. If you use `ChatBrowserUse(model='browser-use/bu-30b-a3b-preview')` with a normal `Agent(...)`, Browser Use still sends its default agent system prompt for you.","","You do **not** need to add a separate custom \"Browser Use system message\" just because you switched to the open-source preview model. Only use `extend_system_message` or `override_system_message` when you intentionally want to customize the default behavior for your task.","","If you want the best default speed/accuracy, we still recommend the newer hosted `bu-*` models. If you want the open-source preview model, the setup stays the same apart from the `model=` value.","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eCan I use custom tools with the agent?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","Yes! You can add custom tools to extend the agent's capabilities:","","```python","from browser_use import Tools","","tools = Tools()","","@tools.action(description='Description of what this tool does.')","def custom_tool(param: str) -\u003e str:","    return f\"Result: {param}\"","","agent = Agent(","    task=\"Your task\",","    llm=llm,","    browser=browser,","    tools=tools,",")","```","","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eCan I use this for free?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","Yes! Browser-Use is open source and free to use. You only need to choose an LLM provider (like OpenAI, Google, ChatBrowserUse, or run local models with Ollama).","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eTerms of Service\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","This open-source library is licensed under the MIT License. For Browser Use services \u0026 data policy, see our [Terms of Service](https://browser-use.com/legal/terms-of-service) and [Privacy Policy](https://browser-use.com/privacy/).","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eHow do I handle authentication?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","Check out our authentication examples:","- [Using real browser profiles](https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/blob/main/examples/browser/real_browser.py) - Reuse your existing Chrome profile with saved logins","- If you want to use temporary accounts with inbox, choose AgentMail","- To sync your auth profile with a remote browser, install `profile-use` for your platform from the [official releases](https://github.com/browser-use/profile-use-releases/releases/latest), then follow the [profile sync guide](https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/interaction-skills/profile-sync.md).","","These examples show how to maintain sessions and handle authentication seamlessly.","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eHow do I solve CAPTCHAs?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","For CAPTCHA handling, you need better browser fingerprinting and proxies. Use [Browser Use Cloud](https://cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github\u0026utm_medium=readme-faq-captcha) which provides stealth browsers designed to avoid detection and CAPTCHA challenges.","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003eHow do I go into production?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e","","Chrome can consume a lot of memory, and running many agents in parallel can be tricky to manage.","","For production use cases, use our [Browser Use Cloud API](https://cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github\u0026utm_medium=readme-faq-production) which handles:","- Scalable browser infrastructure","- Memory management","- Proxy rotation","- Stealth browser fingerprinting","- High-performance parallel execution","\u003c/details\u003e","","\u003cbr/\u003e","","## Citation","","If you use Browser Use in your research or project, please <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What can Browser Use do?</h1><a id="user-content-what-can-browser-use-do" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What can Browser Use do?" href="#what-can-browser-use-do"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Browser Use lets an AI agent use a web browser the same way humans do — it opens pages, clicks buttons, types, and fills in forms. You describe the task, and it completes it. For example, you can have it:</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📋 Fill Forms</h3><a id="user-content--fill-forms" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📋 Fill Forms" href="#-fill-forms"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Task: "Fill in this job application with my resume and information."</h4><a id="user-content-task-fill-in-this-job-application-with-my-resume-and-information" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Task: &quot;Fill in this job application with my resume and information.&quot;" href="#task-fill-in-this-job-application-with-my-resume-and-information"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Start with Browser Use Cloud</h1><a id="user-content-start-with-browser-use-cloud" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Start with Browser Use Cloud" href="#start-with-browser-use-cloud"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Eligible new Google, GitHub or Microsoft signups get a one-time <strong>$15 Cloud credit</strong>. No card required; email/password signups do not qualify. <a href="https://browser-use.com/pricing.md" rel="nofollow">Pricing and eligibility</a>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Hosted agent:</strong> <a href="https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/agent/quickstart" rel="nofollow">V4 quickstart</a>, using <code>browser-use-sdk</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Your own agent:</strong> <a href="https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/browser/quickstart" rel="nofollow">Managed browser quickstart</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Local framework:</strong> the <code>browser-use</code> Python library below.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quickstart</h1><a id="user-content-quickstart" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quickstart" href="#quickstart"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you want to use Browser Use in your agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Hermes, OpenClaw, etc.), paste this prompt, and it sets everything up itself:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Install or upgrade browser-use to the latest stable version with uv using Python 3.12, run `browser-use skill install` to register the skill, and connect it to my browser. If setup or connection fails, follow https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/install.md."><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>Install or upgrade browser-use to the latest stable version with uv using Python 3.12, run `browser-use skill install` to register the skill, and connect it to my browser. If setup or connection fails, follow https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/install.md.
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then tell your agent what you want done.</p>
<br>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Python library: the easiest way to automate the web</h1><a id="user-content-python-library-the-easiest-way-to-automate-the-web" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Python library: the easiest way to automate the web" href="#python-library-the-easiest-way-to-automate-the-web"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Want to automate the web at scale, from your own code, and with any LLM? Use the Python library:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>1. Install Browser Use (Python &gt;= 3.11):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="uv add browser-use
# or: pip install browser-use"><pre>uv add browser-use
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or: pip install browser-use</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>2. Add your LLM API key to <code>.env</code></strong>. Get one from <a href="https://cloud.browser-use.com/new-api-key?utm_source=github&amp;utm_medium=readme-quickstart-api-key" rel="nofollow">Browser Use Cloud</a>, or bring your own provider key:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# .env
BROWSER_USE_API_KEY=your-key
# GOOGLE_API_KEY=your-key
# ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=your-key"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> .env</span>
BROWSER_USE_API_KEY=your-key
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> GOOGLE_API_KEY=your-key</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=your-key</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>3. Run your first agent:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import asyncio

from browser_use import Agent, ChatBrowserUse

async def main():
    agent = Agent(
        task=&quot;Find the number of stars of the browser-use repo&quot;,
        llm=ChatBrowserUse(model='openai/gpt-5.5'),
        # llm=ChatBrowserUse(model='bu-2-0-mini-preview'),  # Browser Use's optimized model
        # llm=ChatOpenAI(model='gpt-5.5'),
        # llm=ChatAnthropic(model='claude-opus-4-8'),  # Sonnet also works well
    )
    history = await agent.run()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    asyncio.run(main())"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>

<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser_use</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Agent</span>, <span class="pl-v">ChatBrowserUse</span>

<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">main</span>():
    <span class="pl-s1">agent</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Agent</span>(
        <span class="pl-s1">task</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Find the number of stars of the browser-use repo"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s1">llm</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">ChatBrowserUse</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'openai/gpt-5.5'</span>),
        <span class="pl-c"># llm=ChatBrowserUse(model='bu-2-0-mini-preview'),  # Browser Use's optimized model</span>
        <span class="pl-c"># llm=ChatOpenAI(model='gpt-5.5'),</span>
        <span class="pl-c"># llm=ChatAnthropic(model='claude-opus-4-8'),  # Sonnet also works well</span>
    )
    <span class="pl-s1">history</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">agent</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>()

<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">__name__</span> <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-s">"__main__"</span>:
    <span class="pl-s1">asyncio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">run</span>(<span class="pl-en">main</span>())</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Check out the <a href="https://docs.browser-use.com/open-source/introduction" rel="nofollow">library docs</a> and the <a href="https://docs.cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github&amp;utm_medium=readme-cloud-docs" rel="nofollow">cloud docs</a> for more!</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Open Source vs Cloud</h1><a id="user-content-open-source-vs-cloud" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Open Source vs Cloud" href="#open-source-vs-cloud"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">We benchmark Browser Use across 100 real-world browser tasks. Full benchmark is open source: <strong><a href="https://github.com/browser-use/benchmark">browser-use/benchmark</a></strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Browser Use is also <strong>#1 on the <a href="https://odysseysbench.com/leaderboard" rel="nofollow">Odysseys leaderboard</a></strong> with an 87.4% average, ahead of computer-use agents from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and Microsoft. Odysseys measures the agent's performance on 200 long-horizon web tasks.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Use the Open-Source Agent</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Free, and runs on your own machine</li>
<li>Deep code-level integration and control: pick your LLM, customize the agent's behavior</li>
<li>We recommend pairing it with our <a href="https://docs.browser-use.com/open-source/customize/browser/remote" rel="nofollow">cloud browsers</a> for leading stealth, proxy rotation, and scaling</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Use the <a href="https://cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github&amp;utm_medium=readme-hosted-agent" rel="nofollow">Fully-Hosted Cloud Agent</a> (recommended)</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Much more powerful agent for complex tasks (see plot above)</li>
<li>Easiest way to start and scale</li>
<li>Best stealth with proxy rotation and captcha solving</li>
<li>1000+ integrations (Gmail, Slack, Notion, and more)</li>
<li>Persistent filesystem and memory</li>
<li>Rerunnable scripts fetch live data, even when sites change (<a href="https://docs.browser-use.com/cloud/agent/scripts" rel="nofollow">guide</a>)</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -X POST https://api.browser-use.com/api/v4/runs \
  -H &quot;X-Browser-Use-API-Key: $BROWSER_USE_API_KEY&quot; \
  -H &quot;Content-Type: application/json&quot; \
  -d '{&quot;task&quot;: &quot;Your task&quot;}'"><pre>curl -X POST https://api.browser-use.com/api/v4/runs \
  -H <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>X-Browser-Use-API-Key: <span class="pl-smi">$BROWSER_USE_API_KEY</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
  -H <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Content-Type: application/json<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
  -d <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>{"task": "Your task"}<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
<br>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Integrations, hosting, custom tools, MCP, and more on our <a href="https://docs.browser-use.com" rel="nofollow">Docs ↗</a></h2><a id="user-content-integrations-hosting-custom-tools-mcp-and-more-on-our-docs-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Integrations, hosting, custom tools, MCP, and more on our Docs ↗" href="#integrations-hosting-custom-tools-mcp-and-more-on-our-docs-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<br>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FAQ</h1><a id="user-content-faq" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FAQ" href="#faq"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<details>
<summary><b>Should I use the CLI vs. the Python library?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Use the CLI</strong> if you already have an agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Hermes, OpenClaw, etc.) that you want to complete browser tasks for you. The agent installs the skill once (see <a href="#quickstart">Quickstart</a>) and can then control the browser. Examples:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>"Upload this video to YouTube"</li>
<li>"Compare these three laptops and give me a table with prices"</li>
<li>"Fill in this job application with my resume"</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Use the Python library</strong> when you are building software that automates the web. Examples:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Run many tasks on a schedule or in parallel (scraping, monitoring, QA)</li>
<li>Embed a browser agent into your own product</li>
<li>Custom tools, custom system prompts, structured output, fine-grained browser control</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Rule of thumb: one-off tasks through an agent → CLI. Repeatable automation in code → Python library.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>What's the best model to use?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">We optimized <strong>ChatBrowserUse()</strong> specifically for browser automation tasks. On avg it completes tasks 3-5x faster than other models with SOTA accuracy.</p>
<p dir="auto">For pricing and other LLM providers, see our <a href="https://docs.browser-use.com/supported-models" rel="nofollow">supported models documentation</a>.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>Can I use Claude / GPT / Gemini through ChatBrowserUse?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes. <code>ChatBrowserUse</code> accepts provider-prefixed model ids, so a single <code>BROWSER_USE_API_KEY</code> reaches all of them — no separate OpenAI/Anthropic/Google keys required:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from browser_use import Agent, ChatBrowserUse

llm = ChatBrowserUse(model='anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6')  # or 'openai/gpt-5.5', 'google/gemini-3-pro'
agent = Agent(task='...', llm=llm)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser_use</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Agent</span>, <span class="pl-v">ChatBrowserUse</span>

<span class="pl-s1">llm</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">ChatBrowserUse</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6'</span>)  <span class="pl-c"># or 'openai/gpt-5.5', 'google/gemini-3-pro'</span>
<span class="pl-s1">agent</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Agent</span>(<span class="pl-s1">task</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'...'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">llm</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">llm</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For the best speed and cost we still recommend the default <code>bu-*</code> models.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>Should I use the Browser Use system prompt with the open-source preview model?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes. If you use <code>ChatBrowserUse(model='browser-use/bu-30b-a3b-preview')</code> with a normal <code>Agent(...)</code>, Browser Use still sends its default agent system prompt for you.</p>
<p dir="auto">You do <strong>not</strong> need to add a separate custom "Browser Use system message" just because you switched to the open-source preview model. Only use <code>extend_system_message</code> or <code>override_system_message</code> when you intentionally want to customize the default behavior for your task.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you want the best default speed/accuracy, we still recommend the newer hosted <code>bu-*</code> models. If you want the open-source preview model, the setup stays the same apart from the <code>model=</code> value.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>Can I use custom tools with the agent?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes! You can add custom tools to extend the agent's capabilities:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from browser_use import Tools

tools = Tools()

@tools.action(description='Description of what this tool does.')
def custom_tool(param: str) -&gt; str:
    return f&quot;Result: {param}&quot;

agent = Agent(
    task=&quot;Your task&quot;,
    llm=llm,
    browser=browser,
    tools=tools,
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser_use</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Tools</span>

<span class="pl-s1">tools</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Tools</span>()

<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">tools</span>.<span class="pl-c1">action</span>(<span class="pl-s1">description</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'Description of what this tool does.'</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">custom_tool</span>(<span class="pl-s1">param</span>: <span class="pl-smi">str</span>) <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-smi">str</span>:
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s">f"Result: <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">param</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>"</span>

<span class="pl-s1">agent</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Agent</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">task</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Your task"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">llm</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">llm</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">browser</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">browser</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">tools</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">tools</span>,
)</pre></div>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>Can I use this for free?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes! Browser-Use is open source and free to use. You only need to choose an LLM provider (like OpenAI, Google, ChatBrowserUse, or run local models with Ollama).</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>Terms of Service</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">This open-source library is licensed under the MIT License. For Browser Use services &amp; data policy, see our <a href="https://browser-use.com/legal/terms-of-service" rel="nofollow">Terms of Service</a> and <a href="https://browser-use.com/privacy/" rel="nofollow">Privacy Policy</a>.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>How do I handle authentication?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">Check out our authentication examples:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/blob/main/examples/browser/real_browser.py">Using real browser profiles</a> - Reuse your existing Chrome profile with saved logins</li>
<li>If you want to use temporary accounts with inbox, choose AgentMail</li>
<li>To sync your auth profile with a remote browser, install <code>profile-use</code> for your platform from the <a href="https://github.com/browser-use/profile-use-releases/releases/latest">official releases</a>, then follow the <a href="https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness/blob/main/interaction-skills/profile-sync.md">profile sync guide</a>.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">These examples show how to maintain sessions and handle authentication seamlessly.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>How do I solve CAPTCHAs?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">For CAPTCHA handling, you need better browser fingerprinting and proxies. Use <a href="https://cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github&amp;utm_medium=readme-faq-captcha" rel="nofollow">Browser Use Cloud</a> which provides stealth browsers designed to avoid detection and CAPTCHA challenges.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>How do I go into production?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">Chrome can consume a lot of memory, and running many agents in parallel can be tricky to manage.</p>
<p dir="auto">For production use cases, use our <a href="https://cloud.browser-use.com?utm_source=github&amp;utm_medium=readme-faq-production" rel="nofollow">Browser Use Cloud API</a> which handles:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Scalable browser infrastructure</li>
<li>Memory management</li>
<li>Proxy rotation</li>
<li>Stealth browser fingerprinting</li>
<li>High-performance parallel execution</li>
</ul>
</details>
<br>
